Core Execution Engine\\n\\n**StarlarkThread** (src/main/java/net/starlark/java/eval/StarlarkThread.java) is the central execution context that manages the evaluation of Starlark code. It maintains thread-local state and manages the call stack during BUILD and .bzl file evaluation.\\n\\n### Environment Setup\\n\\n**BazelStarlarkEnvironment** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/BazelStarlarkEnvironment.java) is responsible for constructing and managing the complete Starlark environment for BUILD files, WORKSPACE files, and .bzl files. It orchestrates three layers of environment setup:\\n\\n1. **StarlarkGlobals** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/StarlarkGlobals.java) - An interface defining the fixed set of top-level symbols available in all Starlark contexts, with implementation in **StarlarkGlobalsImpl** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/analysis/starlark/StarlarkGlobalsImpl.java)\\n2. Additional symbols registered through the ConfiguredRuleClassProvider\\n3. Builtins injection via StarlarkBuiltinsFunction\\n\\n### BUILD File Evaluation Pipeline\\n\\n**PackageFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/PackageFunction.java) is the high-level orchestrator in the Skyframe computation framework that coordinates the entire package loading and evaluation process.\\n\\n**PackageFactory** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/PackageFactory.java) is the primary factory that invokes Starlark evaluation of BUILD files. It:\\n- Creates and configures the StarlarkThread for BUILD file evaluation\\n- Loads the appropriate Starlark environment from BazelStarlarkEnvironment\\n- Manages the Package.Builder for target accumulation\\n- Handles errors and exceptions during evaluation\\n\\n### .bzl File Loading\\n\\n**BzlLoadFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/BzlLoadFunction.java) handles the loading and evaluation of .bzl (Starlark library) files in the Skyframe framework. It manages:\\n- Loading .bzl file source code\\n- Creating Starlark evaluation threads for .bzl files\\n- Caching evaluated .bzl modules\\n- Handling imports between .bzl files\\n\\n**StarlarkBuiltinsFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/StarlarkBuiltinsFunction.java) handles special evaluation of the @_builtins pseudo-repository's .bzl files, which provide injectable builtins that can override or extend the base Starlark environment.\\n\\n### Rule and Macro Definition and Instantiation\\n\\n**StarlarkRuleClassFunctions** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/analysis/starlark/StarlarkRuleClassFunctions.java) is the comprehensive implementation of Starlark rule and macro definitions. It contains:\\n\\n- **rule()** method that creates **StarlarkRuleFunction** instances - these are callable objects that users invoke in BUILD files to instantiate rules. StarlarkRuleFunction implements the call() method that:\\n  - Validates the rule can be instantiated in a BUILD file context\\n  - Retrieves the Package.Builder from the thread's context\\n  - Creates Rule instances via RuleFactory\\n  - Applies rule initializers\\n\\n- **macro()** method that creates **MacroFunction** instances - these are callable objects implementing MacroFunctionApi that users invoke to instantiate symbolic macros. MacroFunction implements the call() method that:\\n  - Validates the macro can be instantiated\\n  - Instantiates MacroClass objects\\n  - Schedules macro evaluation (immediately for non-finalizers, deferred for finalizers)\\n\\n### Macro and Rule Representation\\n\\n**MacroClass** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroClass.java) represents a symbolic macro definition, analogous to RuleClass. It stores:\\n- The macro's implementation function (a StarlarkFunction)\\n- Macro parameters and their defaults\\n- Methods for instantiating MacroInstance objects during BUILD file evaluation\\n\\n**MacroInstance** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroInstance.java) represents a particular instantiation of a macro within a package, similar to how Rule represents a rule instantiation.\\n\\n**RuleFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFunction.java) is an interface that StarlarkRuleFunction implements, bridging the rule definition with the RuleClass.\\n\\n**RuleFactory** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFactory.java) is responsible for creating Rule instances from RuleClass definitions and attribute values provided during rule() invocations in BUILD files.\\n\\n### Package Target Accumulation\\n\\n**Package.Builder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/Package.java, inner class) accumulates targets and macros created during BUILD file evaluation. It:\\n- Maintains a Package.Builder instance in the StarlarkThread's context via Package.Builder.fromOrNull(thread)\\n- Provides methods to add rules, macros, and other targets\\n- Manages the namespace and enforces naming constraints\\n- Delegates to TargetRecorder for target/macro recording\\n\\n**TargetRecorder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/TargetRecorder.java) handles the actual recording of targets and macros within a package. It:\\n- Maintains maps of recorded targets and macro instances\\n- Enforces naming requirements (e.g., macro targets must follow naming conventions)\\n- Prevents name conflicts between targets and macros\\n- Tracks macro scoping and namespace hierarchy\\n\\n### Execution Flow\\n\\nThe evaluation flow proceeds as:\\n1. PackageFunction initiates package evaluation\\n2. PackageFactory creates StarlarkThread and loads environment via BazelStarlarkEnvironment\\n3. BazelStarlarkEnvironment provides base symbols from StarlarkGlobals plus rule/macro functions\\n4. For .bzl file imports encountered during BUILD evaluation, BzlLoadFunction loads and evaluates them\\n5. When BUILD code calls rule() or macro(), StarlarkRuleFunction or MacroFunction are invoked\\n6. These retrieve Package.Builder from thread context and instantiate Rule/MacroInstance objects\\n7. TargetRecorder records these in the package's namespace\\n8. After BUILD evaluation completes, finalizer macros are evaluated\\n9. The completed Package with all targets is returned to the Skyframe framework\"\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":10,"cache_creation_input_tokens":301,"cache_read_input_tokens":56090,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":301,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f95a0136-163f-4960-8c9d-8060f17bd82f","uuid":"945bce9e-2ba2-4914-a679-b99582e93e6f"}

Overview\\n\\n### Core Execution Engine\\n\\n**StarlarkThread** (src/main/java/net/starlark/java/eval/StarlarkThread.java) is the central execution context that manages the evaluation of Starlark code. It maintains thread-local state and manages the call stack during BUILD and .bzl file evaluation.\\n\\n### Environment Setup\\n\\n**BazelStarlarkEnvironment** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/BazelStarlarkEnvironment.java) is responsible for constructing and managing the complete Starlark environment for BUILD files, WORKSPACE files, and .bzl files. It orchestrates three layers of environment setup:\\n\\n1. **StarlarkGlobals** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/StarlarkGlobals.java) - An interface defining the fixed set of top-level symbols available in all Starlark contexts, with implementation in **StarlarkGlobalsImpl** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/analysis/starlark/StarlarkGlobalsImpl.java)\\n2. Additional symbols registered through the ConfiguredRuleClassProvider\\n3. Builtins injection via StarlarkBuiltinsFunction\\n\\n### BUILD File Evaluation Pipeline\\n\\n**PackageFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/PackageFunction.java) is the high-level orchestrator in the Skyframe computation framework that coordinates the entire package loading and evaluation process.\\n\\n**PackageFactory** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/PackageFactory.java) is the primary factory that invokes Starlark evaluation of BUILD files. It:\\n- Creates and configures the StarlarkThread for BUILD file evaluation\\n- Loads the appropriate Starlark environment from BazelStarlarkEnvironment\\n- Manages the Package.Builder for target accumulation\\n- Handles errors and exceptions during evaluation\\n\\n### .bzl File Loading\\n\\n**BzlLoadFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/BzlLoadFunction.java) handles the loading and evaluation of .bzl (Starlark library) files in the Skyframe framework. It manages:\\n- Loading .bzl file source code\\n- Creating Starlark evaluation threads for .bzl files\\n- Caching evaluated .bzl modules\\n- Handling imports between .bzl files\\n\\n**StarlarkBuiltinsFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/skyframe/StarlarkBuiltinsFunction.java) handles special evaluation of the @_builtins pseudo-repository's .bzl files, which provide injectable builtins that can override or extend the base Starlark environment.\\n\\n### Rule and Macro Definition and Instantiation\\n\\n**StarlarkRuleClassFunctions** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/analysis/starlark/StarlarkRuleClassFunctions.java) is the comprehensive implementation of Starlark rule and macro definitions. It contains:\\n\\n- **rule()** method that creates **StarlarkRuleFunction** instances - these are callable objects that users invoke in BUILD files to instantiate rules. StarlarkRuleFunction implements the call() method that:\\n  - Validates the rule can be instantiated in a BUILD file context\\n  - Retrieves the Package.Builder from the thread's context\\n  - Creates Rule instances via RuleFactory\\n  - Applies rule initializers\\n\\n- **macro()** method that creates **MacroFunction** instances - these are callable objects implementing MacroFunctionApi that users invoke to instantiate symbolic macros. MacroFunction implements the call() method that:\\n  - Validates the macro can be instantiated\\n  - Instantiates MacroClass objects\\n  - Schedules macro evaluation (immediately for non-finalizers, deferred for finalizers)\\n\\n### Macro and Rule Representation\\n\\n**MacroClass** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroClass.java) represents a symbolic macro definition, analogous to RuleClass. It stores:\\n- The macro's implementation function (a StarlarkFunction)\\n- Macro parameters and their defaults\\n- Methods for instantiating MacroInstance objects during BUILD file evaluation\\n\\n**MacroInstance** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroInstance.java) represents a particular instantiation of a macro within a package, similar to how Rule represents a rule instantiation.\\n\\n**RuleFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFunction.java) is an interface that StarlarkRuleFunction implements, bridging the rule definition with the RuleClass.\\n\\n**RuleFactory** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFactory.java) is responsible for creating Rule instances from RuleClass definitions and attribute values provided during rule() invocations in BUILD files.\\n\\n### Package Target Accumulation\\n\\n**Package.Builder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/Package.java, inner class) accumulates targets and macros created during BUILD file evaluation. It:\\n- Maintains a Package.Builder instance in the StarlarkThread's context via Package.Builder.fromOrNull(thread)\\n- Provides methods to add rules, macros, and other targets\\n- Manages the namespace and enforces naming constraints\\n- Delegates to TargetRecorder for target/macro recording\\n\\n**TargetRecorder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/TargetRecorder.java) handles the actual recording of targets and macros within a package. It:\\n- Maintains maps of recorded targets and macro instances\\n- Enforces naming requirements (e.g., macro targets must follow naming conventions)\\n- Prevents name conflicts between targets and macros\\n- Tracks macro scoping and namespace hierarchy\\n\\n### Execution Flow\\n\\nThe evaluation flow proceeds as:\\n1. PackageFunction initiates package evaluation\\n2. PackageFactory creates StarlarkThread and loads environment via BazelStarlarkEnvironment\\n3. BazelStarlarkEnvironment provides base symbols from StarlarkGlobals plus rule/macro functions\\n4. For .bzl file imports encountered during BUILD evaluation, BzlLoadFunction loads and evaluates them\\n5. When BUILD code calls rule() or macro(), StarlarkRuleFunction or MacroFunction are invoked\\n6. These retrieve Package.Builder from thread context and instantiate Rule/MacroInstance objects\\n7. TargetRecorder records these in the package's namespace\\n8. After BUILD evaluation completes, finalizer macros are evaluated\\n9. The completed Package with all targets is returned to the Skyframe framework\"\n    49→}\n    50→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. MacroFunction implements the call() method that:\\n  - Validates the macro can be instantiated\\n  - Instantiates MacroClass objects\\n  - Schedules macro evaluation (immediately for non-finalizers, deferred for finalizers)\\n\\n### Macro and Rule Representation\\n\\n**MacroClass** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroClass.java) represents a symbolic macro definition, analogous to RuleClass. It stores:\\n- The macro's implementation function (a StarlarkFunction)\\n- Macro parameters and their defaults\\n- Methods for instantiating MacroInstance objects during BUILD file evaluation\\n\\n**MacroInstance** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/MacroInstance.java) represents a particular instantiation of a macro within a package, similar to how Rule represents a rule instantiation.\\n\\n**RuleFunction** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFunction.java) is an interface that StarlarkRuleFunction implements, bridging the rule definition with the RuleClass.\\n\\n**RuleFactory** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/RuleFactory.java) is responsible for creating Rule instances from RuleClass definitions and attribute values provided during rule() invocations in BUILD files.\\n\\n### Package Target Accumulation\\n\\n**Package.Builder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/Package.java, inner class) accumulates targets and macros created during BUILD file evaluation. It:\\n- Maintains a Package.Builder instance in the StarlarkThread's context via Package.Builder.fromOrNull(thread)\\n- Provides methods to add rules, macros, and other targets\\n- Manages the namespace and enforces naming constraints\\n- Delegates to TargetRecorder for target/macro recording\\n\\n**TargetRecorder** (src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/packages/TargetRecorder.java) handles the actual recording of targets and macros within a package. It:\\n- Maintains maps of recorded targets and macro instances\\n- Enforces naming requirements (e.g., macro targets must follow naming conventions)\\n- Prevents name conflicts between targets and macros\\n- Tracks macro scoping and namespace hierarchy\\n\\n### Execution Flow\\n\\nThe evaluation flow proceeds as:\\n1. PackageFunction initiates package evaluation\\n2. PackageFactory creates StarlarkThread and loads environment via BazelStarlarkEnvironment\\n3. BazelStarlarkEnvironment provides base symbols from StarlarkGlobals plus rule/macro functions\\n4. For .bzl file imports encountered during BUILD evaluation, BzlLoadFunction loads and evaluates them\\n5. When BUILD code calls rule() or macro(), StarlarkRuleFunction or MacroFunction are invoked\\n6. These retrieve Package.Builder from thread context and instantiate Rule/MacroInstance objects\\n7. TargetRecorder records these in the package's namespace\\n8. After BUILD evaluation completes, finalizer macros are evaluated\\n9. The completed Package with all targets is returned to the Skyframe framework\"\n}\n","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":50}}}
